MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS
管理層討論與分析

BUSINESS REVIEW                                                            業務回顧
The Group is a global leading provider of high-tech and innovative         本集團是全球領先的高科技創新產品提供商，
products, providing customers around the world with one-stop               依託電子信息技術、人工智能技術、5G 和物
product solutions relying on its core advantages in electronic             聯網技術、熱管理技術、新材料技術、精密模
information, AI, 5G and Internet of Things, thermal management, new        具技術和數字化製造技術等核心優勢，為全球

billion in 2026, with China contributing approximately US$533 billion.

DIVIDEND DISTRIBUTION POLICY                                                 派付股息政策
The Company seeks to maintain a balance between meeting                      本公司致力通過可持續的股息政策，在符合股
shareholders’ expectations and prudent capital management with               東期望與審慎資本管理兩者之間保持平衡。本
sustainable dividend policy. The Company’s dividend policy aims              公司的股息政策旨在讓股東得以分享本公司的
to allow shareholders to share the Company’s profits and the                 利潤，同時讓本公司預留足夠儲備供日後發展
Company to retain adequate reserves for future growth. In proposing          之用。在建議宣派股息時，本公司會考慮多
any dividend payout, the Company would consider various factors              項因素，包括(i)本集團的財務業績及整體財務
including (i) the financial performance and overall financial position       狀況；(ii)本集團的債務對權益比率及股本回報
of the Group; (ii) the debt-to-equity ratio and return on equity of the      率；(iii)本集團的流動資金狀況及資本需求；(iv)
Group; (iii) the liquidity position and capital requirements of the Group;   本集團的當前及未來營運；(v)本集團的業務發
(iv) the current and future operation of the Group; (v) the business         展策略及未來擴充計劃；(vi)整體市況；(vii)聯
development strategy and future expansion plans of the Group; (vi)           交所證券上市規則（「上市規則」）及適用法例、
the general market conditions; (vii) any relevant requirements of the        規則及規例以及本公司組織章程細則（「章程細
Rules Governing the Listing of Securities (the “Listing Rules”) on the       則」）的任何相關規定；及(viii)董事會認為相關
Stock Exchange and applicable laws, rules and regulations as well as         的任何其他因素。經過詳細論證後，履行相應
the Company’s articles of association (the “Articles of Association”);       的決策程序，末期股息經股東大會批准。在滿
and (viii) any other factors which the Board deems relevant. The             足派息政策條件下，董事會可以根據公司的盈
final dividend was approved at the general meeting after thorough            利狀況及資金需求狀況提議公司進行中期分配。
discussion and compliance with relevant decision-making procedures.
Compliant with the conditions under the dividend distribution policy,
the Board may propose interim dividend distribution based on the
profitability and capital requirements of the Company.

make recommendations to the Board on the remuneration packages            第E.1.2(c)(ii)條所述模式）。執行董事的薪酬組
of individual executive Directors and senior management (adopting         合主要部份包括基本薪金及酌情花紅。本公司
the model described in code provision E.1.2(c)(ii) of the Code). The      並無授予獨立非執行董事與績效掛鈎的股權薪

THE BOARD’S DIVERSITY POLICY                                             董事會成員多元化政策
The Company recognises the importance of diversity to corporate          本公司認同董事會成員多元化對企業管治及董
governance and an effective Board. The Board has, accordingly,           事會行之有效的重要性，董事會因而已採納董

appropriate skills, experience and diverse views necessary for the       會根據本公司業務所需具備適當的技能、經驗
business of the Company. To realise Board diversity, all appointments    及多元化觀點。董事會所有委任均以用人唯才
of the Board members will be made based on merit, and measurable         為原則，將按年討論及協議可計量目標，以落

IDENTIFICATION, EVALUATION AND MANAGEMENT                                識別、評估及管理重大風險
OF MAJOR RISKS
The management team and relevant staff identify risks that may exert     管理層及相關員工識別可能對本公司及運營構
potential impacts on the Company and its operation, and evaluate and     成潛在影響之風險，評估監控環境和流程方面
monitor risks in the environment and process. Through comparison         的風險。通過比較風險評估結果，排列風險優
of the risk appraisal results and risk prioritization, risk management   先次序，釐定風險管理策略及內部監控程序，

LAPSE OF THE FIRST UNLOCKING TRANCHE                                        第一個解鎖期失效

the underlying shares corresponding to the First Unlocking Tranche)         標的股票）將不會解鎖（即不會歸屬）。本公司
granted to the Holders under the 2025 Share Award Scheme will not           將通過信託機構無償收回相關標的股票，用於
be unlocked (i.e. will not vest). The relevant interest in the Underlying   後續股份計劃。
Shares will be recovered by the Company through a trust institution at
no consideration for future share schemes.

ageing, existence of disputes and historic payments, as well 斷及估計。
as forecasts of future economic conditions. The groupings
of various customer segments and the estimation of

Key audit matter
關鍵審核事項

    Inventory provision                                        存貨撥備


As at 31 December 2025, inventories and related provision 於二零二五年十二月三十一日，存貨及相關撥備分別為人民
amounted to RMB18,973,257,000 and RMB491,306,000, 幣18,973,257,000元及人民幣491,306,000元。
respectively.

Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net realisable 存貨按成本與可變現淨值兩者中的較低者列賬。 貴集團存
value. The inventories of the Group are mobile intelligent 貨為產品革新及技術升級迅速的移動智能終端及其他產品，
terminals and other products which are subject to rapid 故廢棄風險極高。管理層對存貨撥備的評估為主觀判斷，乃
product innovations and technological upgrades and 基於（特別是）預測存貨用途、預計售價以及完成及出售產
therefore have a high risk of obsolescence. Management 生的成本所作之假設，而該等假設受預期未來市場及銷售訂
assessment on inventory provision is judgemental and is 單所影響。
based on assumptions, specifically the forecasted inventory
usage, estimated selling prices and cost to be incurred to
completion and disposal, which are affected by expected
future market and sales orders.

We focused on this area due to the magnitude of the 我們關注該範疇乃由於存貨之賬面價值重大及管理層就存貨
carrying amounts of inventories and the fact that significant 撥備應用重大判斷的事實。
judgments on the inventory provision were applied by
management.

The accounting policies and disclosures for inventory 存貨撥備的會計政策及披露載於合併財務報表附註2.4、3及
provision are included in notes 2.4, 3 and 19 to the 19。
consolidated financial statements.

How our audit addressed the key audit matter                   我們於審核時如何處理關鍵審核事項

We performed the following procedures, among others, on 我們就存貨撥備執行以下程序（其中包括）：
the inventory provision:

•     Obtained an understanding of management’s •                   了解計算存貨撥備時管理層所用的方法及估計；
      methodology and estimates used in the inventory
      provision calculation;

•     Assessed the reasonableness of the inventory provision •      與過往撇減、實際售價及銷售成本進行比較，評估存貨
      by comparing it to historical write-downs, actual selling     撥備的合理性；
      prices and costs to sale;

•     Tested the ageing of inventories and discussed the •          測試存貨的賬齡及與管理層討論賬齡較長的存貨，以識
      long-aged inventories with management to identify any         別出任何滯銷、多餘或廢棄產品；
      slow-moving, excess or obsolete items;

        such control ceases.

         a cross-reference to HKFRS 18 for entities that use these                關的披露要求；及(iii)將與管理層界定

  2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                       2.4 重大會計政策
     Business combinations and goodwill                                    業務合併及商譽
     Business combinations are accounted for using the acquisition         業務合併乃以收購法入賬。轉讓的代
     method. The consideration transferred is measured at the              價乃以收購日期的公允價值計量，該
     acquisition date fair value which is the sum of the acquisition       公允價值為本集團轉讓的資產、本集
     date fair values of assets transferred by the Group, liabilities      團自被收購方的前擁有人承擔的負債
     assumed by the Group to the former owners of the acquiree             及本集團發行以換取被收購方控制權
     and the equity interests issued by the Group in exchange for          的股本權益於收購日期的公允價值總
     control of the acquiree. For each business combination, the           和。於各業務合併中，本集團選擇是
     Group elects whether to measure the non-controlling interests         否以公允價值或被收購方可識別資產
     in the acquiree at fair value or at the proportionate share of        淨值的應佔比例，計量於被收購方的
     the acquiree’s identifiable net assets. All other components          非控股權益。非控股權益的所有其他
     of non-controlling interests are measured at fair value.              組成部分乃以公允價值計量。收購相
     Acquisition-related costs are expensed as incurred.                   關成本於產生時列為開支。


     The Group determines that it has acquired a business when             當所購入的一系列業務及資產包括一
     the acquired set of activities and assets includes an input and       項投入及一項實質性流程，共同對創
     a substantive process that together significantly contribute to       造產出的能力作出重大貢獻時，本集
     the ability to create outputs.                                        團確定其已收購一項業務。


     When the Group acquires a business, it assesses the financial         當本集團收購一項業務時，會根據合
     assets and liabilities assumed for appropriate classification         同條款、於收購日期的經濟環境及相
     and designation in accordance with the contractual terms,             關條件，評估將承擔的金融資產及負
     economic circumstances and pertinent conditions as at the             債，以作出適合的分類及指定，其中
     acquisition date. This includes the separation of embedded            包括將被收購方主合同中的嵌入式衍
     derivatives in host contracts of the acquiree.                        生工具進行分離。


     If the business combination is achieved in stages, the                倘業務合併分階段進行，先前持有的
     previously held equity interest is remeasured at its acquisition      股權按收購日期的公允價值重新計
     date fair value and any resulting gain or loss is recognised in       量，而任何所得收益或虧損則於損益
     profit or loss.                                                       中確認。


     Any contingent consideration to be transferred by the acquirer        收購方所轉讓的任何或然代價按於收
     is recognised at fair value at the acquisition date. Contingent       購日期的公允價值確認。分類為資產
     consideration classified as an asset or liability is measured         或負債的或然代價以公允價值計量，
     at fair value with changes in fair value recognised in profit or      公允價值變動於損益內確認。分類為
     loss. Contingent consideration that is classified as equity is        權益的或然代價並無重新計量，而其
     not remeasured and subsequent settlement is accounted for             後結算於權益中入賬。
     within equity.

     Fair value measurement                                                   公允價值計量
     The Group measures its equity investments and derivative                 本集團於各報告期末以公允價值計量
     financial instruments at fair value at the end of each reporting         其股本投資及衍生金融工具。公允價
     period. Fair value is the price that would be received to sell           值指市場參與者於計量日期在有序交
     an asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction       易中出售資產所收取或轉讓負債所支
     between market participants at the measurement date. The                 付的價格。公允價值計量所依據的假
     fair value measurement is based on the presumption that the              設為，出售資產或轉讓負債的交易乃
     transaction to sell the asset or transfer the liability takes place      於該資產或負債的主要市場進行，或
     either in the principal market for the asset or liability, or in         如無主要市場，則於對該資產或負債
     the absence of a principal market, in the most advantageous              最為有利的市場進行。本集團必須可
     market for the asset or liability. The principal or the most             進入該主要或最為有利的市場。計量
     advantageous market must be accessible by the Group.                     資產或負債的公允價值使用市場參與
     The fair value of an asset or a liability is measured using the          者於為該資產或負債定價時所依據的
     assumptions that market participants would use when pricing              假設，即假設市場參與者按其最佳經
     the asset or liability, assuming that market participants act in         濟利益行事。
     their economic best interest.


     A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into             非金融資產的公允價值計量參考市場
     account a market participant’s ability to generate economic              參與者可從使用該資產得到的最高及
     benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or by            最佳效用，或把該資產售予另一可從
     selling it to another market participant that would use the              使用該資產得到最高及最佳效用的市
     asset in its highest and best use.                                       場參與者所產生的經濟效益。


     The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate                 本集團使用適用於不同情況的估值方
     in the circumstances and for which sufficient data are                   法，而其有足夠數據可計量公允價
     available to measure fair value, maximising the use of relevant          值，並盡量利用相關可觀察輸入數據
     observable inputs and minimising the use of unobservable                 及盡量減少使用不可觀察輸入數據。
     inputs.

        Depreciation is calculated on the straight-line basis to write               每項物業、廠房及設備項目的折舊乃
        off the cost of each item of property, plant and equipment to                以直線法按其估計可使用年期攤銷其
        its residual value over its estimated useful life. The principal             成本至其剩餘價值計算。主要估計可

                                                  估計可使用年期                                                剩餘價值


        Freehold land                             Not depreciated                                               –
        永久業權土地                                    並無折舊
        Buildings                                 5 to 70 years                                           0%-5%

        Leasehold improvements                    Shorter of lease term or estimated useful life                –
        租賃物業裝修                                    租賃期與估計可使用年期孰短
        Machinery and equipment                   3 to 12 years                                           0%-5%
        機器及設備                                     3至12年                                                  0%至5%
        Office equipment and fixtures             10 years and below                                      0%-5%
        辦公室設備及傢俱                                  10年及以下                                                 0%至5%
        Motor vehicles                            3 to 5 years                                            0%-5%
        車輛                                        3至5年                                                   0%至5%

        Where parts of an item of property, plant and equipment                      倘物業、廠房及設備項目各部分的可
        have different useful lives, the cost of that item is allocated              使用年期並不相同，該項目各部分的
        on a reasonable basis among the parts and each part                          成本將按合理基礎分配，而各部分將
        is depreciated separately. Residual values, useful lives                     作個別折舊。剩餘價值、可使用年期
        and depreciation methods are reviewed, and adjusted if                       及折舊方法至少於各財政年度年底審
        appropriate, at least at each financial year end.                            核，並在適當情況下加以調整。


        An item of property, plant and equipment including any                       包括已初步確認的任何重要部分的物
        significant part initially recognised is derecognised upon                   業、廠房及設備項目於出售時或預計

            Right-of-use assets are depreciated on a straight-line                用權資產按直線基準於租賃期及該

     Impairment of financial assets                                           金融資產減值
     The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit losses             本集團確認對並非持作以公允價值計
     (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair value through         量並計入損益的所有債務工具的預期
     profit or loss. ECLs are based on the difference between the             信貸虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）的一項撥
     contractual cash flows due in accordance with the contract               備。預期信貸虧損乃基於根據合同到
     and all the cash flows that the Group expects to receive,                期的合同現金流量與本集團預期收取
     discounted at an approximation of the original effective                 的所有現金流量之間的差額而釐定，
     interest rate. The expected cash flows will include cash flows           並以原實際利率的近似值折現。預期
     from the sale of collateral held or other credit enhancements            現金流量將包括出售所持抵押的現金
     that are integral to the contractual terms.                              流量或組成合同條款的其他信貸提升
                                                                              措施的現金流量。


     General approach                                                         一般方式
     ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures for              預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。就初
     which there has not been a significant increase in credit risk           始確認以來信貸風險並無大幅增加的
     since initial recognition, ECLs are provided for credit losses           信貸敞口而言，會為未來12 個月內
     that result from default events that are possible within the next        （12 個月預期信貸虧損）可能發生的違
     12 months (a 12-month ECL). For those credit exposures for               約事件所產生的信貸虧損計提預期信
     which there has been a significant increase in credit risk since         貸虧損撥備。就初始確認以來信貸風
     initial recognition, a loss allowance is required for credit losses      險大幅增加的信貸敞口而言，須就預
     expected over the remaining life of the exposure, irrespective           期於敞口的餘下年期產生的信貸虧損
     of the timing of the default (a lifetime ECL).                           計提虧損撥備，不論違約的時間（年限
                                                                              內預期信貸虧損）。


     At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether the                   於各報告日期，本集團評估於初始確
     credit risk on a financial instrument has increased significantly        認時金融工具的信貸風險是否顯著增
     since initial recognition. When making the assessment,                   加。於進行評估時，本集團將金融工
     the Group compares the risk of a default occurring on the                具於報告日期發生的違約風險與金融
     financial instrument as at the reporting date with the risk of a         工具於初始確認日期發生的違約風險
     default occurring on the financial instrument as at the date of          進行比較，並考慮無需過多成本或努
     initial recognition and considers reasonable and supportable             力即可得之合理且可證明的資料，包
     information that is available without undue cost or effort,              括歷史及前瞻性資料。
     including historical and forward-looking information.

     Derecognition of financial liabilities                                  終止確認金融負債
     A financial liability is derecognised when the obligation under         金融負債於負債的責任已解除或註銷
     the liability is discharged or cancelled, or expires.                   或屆滿時終止確認。


     When an existing financial liability is replaced by another             當現有金融負債為同一貸款人以實質
     from the same lender on substantially different terms, or the           上不同條款的負債取代時，或現有負
     terms of an existing liability are substantially modified, such         債的條款有重大修改時，有關交換或
     an exchange or modification is treated as a derecognition of            修改則被視為終止確認原有負債及確
     the original liability and a recognition of a new liability, and        認一項新負債，而有關賬面價值的差
     the difference between the respective carrying amounts is               額乃於損益表確認。
     recognised in the statement of profit or loss.


     Offsetting of financial instruments                                     抵銷金融工具
     Financial assets and financial liabilities are offset and the           倘於現時具有可強制執行的法定權利
     net amount is reported in the statement of financial position           可抵銷已確認金額及有意按淨額基準
     if there is a currently enforceable legal right to offset the           結算，或同時變現資產及清償負債
     recognised amounts and there is an intention to settle on               時，金融資產及金融負債方會予以抵
     a net basis, or to realise the assets and settle the liabilities        銷並於財務狀況表內呈報淨額。
     simultaneously.

     Inventories                                                           存貨
     Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net realisable        存貨按成本與可變現淨值兩者中的較
     value. Cost is determined on the weighted average basis and,          低者列賬。成本按加權平均基準釐

     of overheads. Cost of moulds is determined at the actual cost         常費用。模具成本按生產過程產生的
     incurred in the production process. Net realisable value is           實際成本釐定。可變現淨值根據估計
     based on estimated selling prices less any estimated costs to         售價減完成及出售所產生的任何估計
     be incurred to completion and disposal.                               成本釐定。

     highly liquid deposits with a maturity of generally within three      期現金承諾而持有、通常於三個月內
     months that are readily convertible into known amounts of             到期、可隨時轉換為已知金額現金且

     Provisions                                                            撥備
     A provision is recognised when a present obligation (legal or         倘因過去事件而須承擔現有責任（法定
     constructive) has arisen as a result of a past event and it is        或推定），且很可能須以日後資源流出
     probable that a future outflow of resources will be required to       清償此等責任，且惟責任金額可作出
     settle the obligation, provided that a reliable estimate can be       可靠估計時，則會確認撥備。
     made of the amount of the obligation.


     The Group provides for warranties in relation to the sale             本集團就銷售特定工業產品及擔保期
     of certain industrial products for general repairs of defects         間產生瑕疵的一般性維修提供擔保。

     recognised based on sales volume and past experience of the           初步確認入賬，並折現至其現值（倘適
     level of repairs and returns, discounted to their present values      用）。擔保相關成本每年予以修訂。
     as appropriate. The warranty related cost is revised annually.

  Principal versus agent considerations                                      主要責任人與代理人考慮事項
  The Group sells products to a customer who is also the supplier            本集團向特定客戶銷售產品，該客戶同時
  of key materials used in the manufacturing of products. The                也是產品製造過程中使用的關鍵材料的供
  Group needs to determine whether it is a principal or an agent             應商。本集團需要通過評估其對客戶的承
  in these transactions by evaluating the nature of its promise to           諾的性質來判斷其在該類交易中是主要責
  the customer. The Group is a principal and therefore records               任人還是代理人。倘本集團於向客戶轉讓
  revenue on a gross basis if it controls promised goods before              商品前控制所承諾商品，則為主要責任
  transferring the goods to the customer. Otherwise, the Group is            人，故須按總額基準記錄收入。否則，
  an agent and records as revenue the net amount that it retains for         本集團為代理人，將收入列為就其代理服
  its agency services if its role is to arrange to provide the goods.        務而保留的淨額（倘其責任為安排提供商
  To assess whether the Group controls the goods before they are             品）。為評估本集團是否在商品轉移給客戶
  transferred to the customer, the Group has considered various              之前控制商品，本集團已考慮各種因素，
  factors, including but not limited to whether the Group (i) is the         包括但不限於本集團是否 (i) 是交易安排中
  primary obligor in the arrangement, (ii) has general inventory             的主要責任人，(ii)承擔存貨的一般風險，
  risk, (iii) has latitude in establishing the selling price and (iv) has    (iii)有權自主決定售價和(iv)對商品的產出和
  significant involvement in the determination of product and service        服務規範有重大投入。

  the recognition of these losses as deferred tax assets. On this            會能部分支持確認該等虧損為遞延稅項資
  basis, the Group has determined that it cannot recognise deferred          產。基於此，本集團釐定無法就結轉稅項
  tax assets on the tax losses carried forward.                              虧損確認遞延稅項資產。

      makes provision for slow-moving items and obsolescence by using             現淨值兩者中較低者對滯銷及廢棄產品作

      Leases – Estimating the incremental borrowing rate                          租賃 － 估算增量借款利率
      The Group cannot readily determine the interest rate implicit in a          本集團無法輕易釐定租賃內所隱含的利
      lease, and therefore, it uses an incremental borrowing rate (“IBR”)         率，因此，使用增量借款利率計量租賃負
      to measure lease liabilities. The IBR is the rate of interest that the      債。增量借款利率為本集團於類似經濟環
      Group would have to pay to borrow over a similar term, and with a           境中為取得與使用權資產價值相近之資
      similar security, the funds necessary to obtain an asset of a similar       產，而以類似抵押品於類似期間借入所需
      value to the right-of-use asset in a similar economic environment.          資金應支付之利率。因此，增量借款利率
      The IBR therefore reflects what the Group “would have to                    反映了本集團「應支付」的利率，當無可觀
      pay”, which requires estimation when no observable rates are                察的利率時（如就並無訂立融資交易之附屬
      available (such as for subsidiaries that do not enter into financing        公司而言）或當須對利率進行調整以反映租
      transactions) or when it needs to be adjusted to reflect the terms          賃之條款及條件時（如當租賃並非以附屬公
      and conditions of the lease (for example, when leases are not in            司的功能貨幣計價時），則須作出估計。當
      the subsidiary’s functional currency). The Group estimates the              可觀察輸入數據可用時，本集團使用可觀
      IBR using observable inputs (such as market interest rates) when            察輸入數據（如市場利率）估算增量借款利
      available and is required to make certain entity-specific estimates         率，並須針對不同實體作出若干估計（例如
      (such as the subsidiary’s stand-alone credit rating).                       附屬公司的單獨信貸評級）。

11. INCOME TAX                                                           11. 所得稅
  The Group is subject to income tax on an entity basis on profits         本集團須就本集團成員公司所處及經營的
  arising in or derived from the jurisdictions in which members of the     司法權區所產生或賺取的溢利，按實體基
  Group are domiciled and operate.                                         準繳納所得稅。


  Under the relevant income tax law, the PRC subsidiaries are              根據相關所得稅法，年內，中國附屬公司
  subject to corporate income tax (“CIT”) at a statutory rate of 25%       須就其各自的應課稅收入按法定稅率25%
  on their respective taxable income during the year.                      繳納企業所得稅（「企業所得稅」）。


  BYD Precision renewed its status of a high and new technology            比亞迪精密於二零二四年重續為高新技術
  enterprise in 2024, and was entitled to a reduced enterprise             企業，並自二零二四年至二零二六年期間
  income tax rate of 15% from 2024 to 2026.                                有權享受15%的優惠企業所得稅稅率。


  Huizhou Electronic renewed its status of a high and new                  惠州電子於二零二四年重續為高新技術企
  technology enterprise in 2024, and was entitled to a reduced             業，並自二零二四年至二零二六年期間有
  enterprise income tax rate of 15% from 2024 to 2026.                     權享受15%的優惠企業所得稅稅率。


  Xi’an Electronic which operates in the Chinese mainland was              西安電子於中國內地經營業務，有權根據
  entitled to a reduced enterprise income tax rate of 15% of the           西部大開發政策按年內估計應課稅溢利享
  estimated assessable profits for the year pursuant to the Western        受15%的優惠企業所得稅稅率。
  Development Policy.

11. INCOME TAX (Continued)                                                   11. 所得稅（續）
      The Group is within the scope of the Pillar Two model rules.             本集團屬於支柱二立法模版範圍。本集團

  IMPAIRMENT TESTING OF GOODWILL                                        商譽之減值測試
  Goodwill acquired through business combinations is allocated          透過業務合併收購的商譽乃分配至Juno
  to the Juno Newco Target Holdco Singapore Pte. Ltd. (“Juno            Newco Target Holdco Singapore Pte. Ltd.
  Newco”) and its subsidiaries as a cash-generating unit for           （「Juno Newco」）及其附屬公司作為現金
  impairment testing:                                                   產生單位以進行減值測試：

39. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT OBJECTIVES                                 39. 財務風險管理目標及政策
    AND POLICIES
  The Group’s principal financial instruments, other than derivatives,    本集團的主要金融工具（衍生工具除外）包
  comprise loans, balances with related companies and cash and            括貸款、與關連公司的結餘以及現金及銀
  bank balances. The main purpose of these financial instruments is       行結餘。此等金融工具的主要用途乃為本
  to raise finance for the Group’s operations. The Group has various      集團的營運籌集資金。本集團有其他不同
  other financial assets and liabilities such as trade receivables and    金融資產及負債，如直接自其營運產生的
  trade payables, which arise directly from its operations.               應收貿易款項及應付貿易賬款。


  The main risks arising from the Group’s financial instruments are       本集團金融工具所產生的主要風險為外匯
  foreign currency risk, credit risk and liquidity risk. The board of     風險、信貸風險及流動資金風險。董事會
  directors reviews and agrees policies for managing each of these        審閱並同意下文所概述的管理各項此等風
  risks and they are summarised below.                                    險的政策。


  INTEREST RATE RISK                                                      利率風險
  At 31 December 2025 and 2024, the Group has no significant              於二零二五年及二零二四年十二月三十一
  interest risk as the Group did not have long-term debt obligations      日，本集團並無重大利率風險，乃因為本
  with floating interest rates.                                           集團並無浮息長期債務責任。


  FOREIGN CURRENCY RISK                                                   外匯風險
  The Group has transactional currency exposures. Such exposures          本集團有交易貨幣風險。有關風險來自經
  arise from sales or purchases by operating units in currencies          營單位以並非該單位功能貨幣的貨幣進行
  other than the units’ functional currencies. The Group tends to         買賣。本集團傾向於在訂立買賣合同時接
  accept foreign currency exchange risk avoidance or allocation           納外匯風險規避或分配條款。本集團對外
  terms when arriving at purchase and sale contracts. The Group           匯收入及開支實行滾動預測，並匹配貨幣
  takes rolling forecast on the foreign currency revenue and              與費用金額，以減輕匯率波動對業務產生
  expenses and matches the currency and the amount incurred               的影響。
  so as to alleviate the impact on business due to exchange rate
  fluctuations.

      CAPITAL MANAGEMENT                                                          資本管理
      The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management are to             本集團資本管理的主要目標為確保本集團
      safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern and            持續發展的能力及保持良好的資本比率，
      to maintain healthy capital ratios in order to support its business         以支持本集團的業務及盡量提升股東價值。
      and maximise shareholders’ value.


      The Group manages its capital structure and makes adjustments               本集團根據經濟狀況的變化管理其資本
      to it in light of changes in economic conditions. To maintain or            架構並作出調整。為保持或調整資本架
      adjust the capital structure, the Group may adjust the dividend             構，本集團或會調整派付予股東的股息、
      payment to shareholders, return capital to shareholders or issue            股東的資本回報或發行新股。截至二零
      new shares. No changes were made in the objectives, policies                二五年十二月三十一日及二零二四年十二
      or processes for managing capital during the years ended 31                 月三十一日止年度，並無對管理資本的目
      December 2025 and 31 December 2024.                                         標、政策或程序作出更改。


      The Group monitors capital using a gearing ratio, which is net              本集團以資本負債比率（即債務淨值除以權
      debt divided by equity. The Group’s policy is to maintain the               益）監察其資本。本集團的政策為將資本負
      gearing ratio as low as possible.                                           債比率盡可能保持最低。

41. TRANSFERS OF FINANCIAL ASSETS                                          41. 金融資產轉移
  FINANCIAL ASSETS THAT ARE DERECOGNISED IN                                  完全撤銷確認的金融資產
  THEIR ENTIRETY
  In the ordinary course of business, the Group has factored a               本集團在日常業務中，因短期融資需求將
  part of receivables to financial institutions without recourse for its     部分應收賬款以無追索權方式保理給金融
  short-term financing needs, and has entered into non-recourse              機構，並與多家銀行訂立無追索權的應收
  receivables factoring agreements with a number of banks to                 賬款保理協議，將若干應收賬款轉讓予該
  transfer certain receivables to those banks (the “Receivable               等 銀 行（「 應 收 賬 款 保 理 」）。 在 若 干 應 收
  Factoring”). Under certain receivable factoring agreements,                賬款保理協議下，本集團無須承擔應收賬
  the Group is not required to undertake default risks and the               款轉讓後的債務人違約風險及延遲還款
  delayed repayment risk from the debtors after the transfer of              風險，且已轉移應收賬款所有權之所有的
  receivables, and all risks and rewards related to the ownership            風險和報酬，符合終止金融資產條件。因
  of the receivables are transferred. The definition of termination of       此，本集團對保理協議下的應收賬款按照
  financial assets is met. Therefore, the Group derecognised the             賬面價值撤銷確認。
  receivables under the factoring agreements at carrying amount.

  As at 31 December 2025, the carrying amount of transferred                 於二零二五年十二月三十一日，根據相
  receivables under the relevant factoring agreements amounted               關保理協議轉讓的應收賬款賬面值合計
  to RMB7,980,691,000 (2024: Nil), and the loss related to                   人民幣 7,980,691,000 元（二零二四年：
